# Test source

```ts
  1  | import { test, expect } from '@playwright/test';
  2  | import { loginAsAdmin } from '../helpers/auth';
  3  | import { execSync } from 'child_process';
  4  | 
  5  | let workspaceId: string = '';
  6  | 
  7  | test.describe('Zahnbürste SysEng Demo', () => {
  8  |   test.beforeAll(async () => {
  9  |     // Run the Python seeding script
  10 |     console.log('Running seed_toothbrush.py...');
  11 |     const output = execSync('docker-compose exec -T backend python seed_toothbrush.py', { encoding: 'utf-8' });
  12 |     
  13 |     // Extract workspace ID
  14 |     const match = output.match(/Workspace created with ID: ([a-f0-9-]+)/);
  15 |     if (!match) {
  16 |       console.error(output);
  17 |       throw new Error('Could not find Workspace ID in seed script output');
  18 |     }
  19 |     workspaceId = match[1];
  20 |     console.log(`Seeded Workspace ID: ${workspaceId}`);
  21 |   });
  22 | 
  23 |   test.beforeEach(async ({ page }) => {
  24 |     await loginAsAdmin(page);
  25 |     // Navigate directly to the seeded workspace
  26 |     await page.goto(`/workspaces/${workspaceId}`);
  27 |   });
  28 | 
  29 |   test('should render the Requirement hierarchy', async ({ page }) => {
  30 |     await page.click('text=Requirements');
  31 |     // Wait for the requirements to load
  32 |     await expect(page.locator('text=Der C-Code für das OTA-Modul muss MISRA-C kompatibel sein.').first()).toBeVisible({ timeout: 30000 });
  33 |     // In Card view, the hierarchy is flattened or shown differently, just check existence of L2 element
  34 |     // await expect(page.locator('text=Die MCU muss den PWM-Kanal 1 für die Motorsteuerung verwenden.').first()).toBeVisible();
  35 |   });
  36 | 
  37 |   test('should render the Architecture tree', async ({ page }) => {
  38 |     await page.click('text=Architecture');
  39 |     await expect(page.locator('text=Smart Toothbrush System').first()).toBeVisible({ timeout: 30000 });
  40 |     await expect(page.locator('text=Handstück').first()).toBeVisible();
  41 |     await expect(page.locator('text=Bürstenkopf').first()).toBeVisible();
  42 |   });
  43 | 
  44 |   test('should render ICDs', async ({ page }) => {
  45 |     // The link was renamed to "ICDs"
```
